Campaign #2088: Cold PSO compilation to the first playable frame (NVIDIA)

https://github.com/xcl-dev/xcl/issues/2088 · updated 2026-11-28 15:19 UTC () · typed JSON for agents
Unofficial draft. Tasks and hardware classes were inferred from the thread by a contributor, until the maintainer adopts or corrects the campaign file. Opened on GitHub by bmjask (a contributor) as a child of #1269; the maintainer shared it on the project's Discord. The issue asks for NVIDIA driver times (Create*PipelineState), not translation or dxil-spirv times.

110authors
546declare AI use

What can I do?

Pick your machine to see the measurements that still need it and the free code tasks.

NVIDIA, Windows

Every measurement has enough machines of this class. Reproducing one on the reference commit is still useful.

Free code tasks (any machine)

  • A13 Port amd-dev XCL_RT_GATE hold mode to nvidia-dev (opt-in)

Code tasks whose holders went quiet (over 48 h): ask in the thread before taking them

  • C4 WS3: nested direct compact dispatch (outer group switch, ~32 classes per group)
  • C6 WS5: generated-DXIL simplification (fewer local arrays, blocks and bytes)
  • C7 WS5: geometry shaders with one-triangle output: SSA value instead of local array + copy loop
  • C8 WS5: pixel-shader PRELUDE::rla() without dynamic indexing (recomp/emit.rs)
  • C9 WS5: memory-window address math in 32-bit, no i64 ops (dxil/lower_window.rs)
  • C10 WS5: direct DXIL raw stores through root UAV memory windows (one-hot guards)
  • C11 WS6: cold benchmark script by worker count (cache cleared, per-build driver time)
  • A1 kernelx: XCL_PAGE_ALIAS real page mappings for queue GPU views
  • A2 WS5: correctness gate for heap-window verification and hardening
  • A3 Recompiler: swizzled buffer V# dword-interleaved addressing (both paths)
  • A4 Keep driver pipeline blob cache on device-removed create failure
  • A5 Direct DXIL geometry ES inputs loaded from heap window
  • A7 Expose actual disk-cache filename in opt-in PSO traces (analysis only)
  • A9 Show driver PSO build count in window title and log
  • A10 Docs: README on translation-only precompile and Developer Mode for measure

The claim line goes at the end of your comment: it shows as one small link, and the board reads the task and the class from it. Results: see AGENTS-campaign.md.

Chips: holders (a team = people linked by an explicit hand-over in the thread); ○ = announced a plan. stale? = no activity on the task for over 48 h. PRs: written by someone working on the task, or cited by 3+ people on it (what was tested); the others are folded under mentioned.

What the numbers say

For each measurement, the few numbers that compare across machines. An AI only points at which reported numbers form a metric; the value is computed by code (units converted, ratios and per-KB rates from the reported numbers). Per cell: the median over machines (one value per person, their own median), the range, and agree when the machines are within ×1.5 of each other. ≠ = the numbers come from different commits.
